### EBPM-047 — Feedback that can expose omitted work

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-047`. Disposition: `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`. Examination: `EXAMINED`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/46`.

**Mature form.** Keep a route by which observations outside the current model—complaints, practitioner accounts, recipient experience and unlogged work—can challenge both the representation and the success measure. Do not let the model define all admissible evidence about its own adequacy.

**Controlled failure.** Self-confirming metrics, inaccessible complaint channels, cherry-picked external evidence and excessive investigative burden.

**Trigger.** A process is evaluated primarily through records generated by its existing model or system.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** Occasional independent case walkthroughs and accessible complaint routes may suffice; exhaustive surveillance is not required.

**Prerequisites.** A channel for discrepant evidence, willingness to review it and access to actors outside the modelling team.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A process is evaluated primarily through records generated by its existing model or system.; consumer and authority: Evidence contributors may challenge a model; revision remains an authorised decision rather than automatic acceptance of every complaint.

**Authority boundary.** Evidence contributors may challenge a model; revision remains an authorised decision rather than automatic acceptance of every complaint.

**Required communication.** Bring external or omitted observations to the people controlling the model, measures and process.

**Observable postcondition.** A material discrepancy can lead to changed inquiry or explicit adjudication rather than exclusion as out of scope by definition.

**Criticism.** S041 shows formal descriptions can be useful while omitting performed work; S025 gives contextual workarounds, not a universal frequency estimate.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The process plus the boundary defining what its model and measures can observe.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Performers, recipients, excluded participants, analysts and owners.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Contradiction between work and representation is admissible evidence, not automatically a defect in the worker.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** A discrepancy account and revised inquiry; a larger formal model is not always the answer.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Omitted burden, unresolved complaints and whether model-based decisions remain warranted.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Keep a route by which observations outside the current model—complaints, practitioner accounts, recipient experience and unlogged work—can challenge both the representation and the success measure. **HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Manual exceptions and informal coordination can reveal missing functions.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Logs and AI summaries inherit the observability limits of their sources.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** Credible contradiction, unexplained outcome or evidence of systematic omission.

### EBPM-048 — Negotiated inter-organisational governance

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-048`. Disposition: `CONTEXT_DEPENDENT`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/47`.

**Mature form.** Negotiate shared outcomes, interfaces, responsibilities and dispute handling among autonomous organisations. Make differences in incentives, information and decision rights explicit; no single process diagram grants one participant authority over the network.

**Controlled failure.** Dominant-party cost shifting; incompatible incentives; unavailable evidence; ambiguous shared ownership.

**Trigger.** A received outcome depends on independent organisations with their own commitments.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** A clear bilateral interface and escalation agreement may suffice for a simple relationship.

**Prerequisites.** Willing counterparties, defined commitments, compatible completion meanings and feasible communication.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A received outcome depends on independent organisations with their own commitments.; consumer and authority: Each organisation can commit only within its mandate; joint decisions require agreed governance rather than assumed central control.

**Authority boundary.** Each organisation can commit only within its mandate; joint decisions require agreed governance rather than assumed central control.

**Required communication.** Exchange interface expectations, capacity issues, rejected handoffs and unresolved cross-party obligations.

**Observable postcondition.** A cross-organisational issue has an agreed decision route and responsibilities visible to participating parties.

**Criticism.** S028’s design-science artefact was evaluated in logistics/port settings but does not establish general network performance improvement.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The shared outcome and participating organisations’ interfaces, not a fictitious unified hierarchy.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Partners, their workers, recipients and parties affected by network delays or failures.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Inspect both parties’ performed handoffs rather than only the initiating organisation’s account.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** Interaction, obligation and governance model with separate organisational boundaries.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Shared outcome, partner burdens, rejected handoffs and dispute resolution.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Negotiate shared outcomes, interfaces, responsibilities and dispute handling among autonomous organisations. Make differences in incentives, information and decision rights explicit; no single process diagram grants one participant authority over the network.; causal limit: S028’s design-science artefact was evaluated in logistics/port settings but does not establish general network performance improvement.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** Each organisation can commit only within its mandate; joint decisions require agreed governance rather than assumed central control.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Local discretion may be constrained differently in each organisation.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Interoperability does not by itself align incentives or establish authority.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** Partner change, disputed completion, shifted burden or failure of agreed escalation.

**Evidence a later target must supply.** Inspect both parties’ performed handoffs rather than only the initiating organisation’s account. Measure: Shared outcome, partner burdens, rejected handoffs and dispute resolution.

### EBPM-049 — Shared-data meaning and disclosure boundaries

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-049`. Disposition: `CONTEXT_DEPENDENT`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/48`.

**Mature form.** Reconcile the meanings of cases, events, messages and completion across interfaces while limiting disclosure to a justified purpose. Technical schema compatibility is not enough when organisations interpret the same field differently.

**Controlled failure.** False equivalence of event labels; inconsistent case identity; privacy leakage; technically valid but misleading joins.

**Trigger.** Data or messages cross organisational/system boundaries or are joined for analysis.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** Agree a small set of shared terms and examples when broad semantic standardisation would add no value.

**Prerequisites.** Identifiable data producers and consumers, access authority and a means of resolving semantic disagreement.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. Data or messages cross organisational/system boundaries or are joined for analysis.; consumer and authority: Data custodians control authorised disclosure; process parties jointly resolve business meaning within their commitments.

**Authority boundary.** Data custodians control authorised disclosure; process parties jointly resolve business meaning within their commitments.

**Required communication.** Communicate definitions, provenance, uncertainty and changes that affect receiving interpretations.

**Observable postcondition.** A receiving actor can interpret the shared evidence for the intended decision, with missing or incompatible meaning explicit.

**Criticism.** S035 highlights event-data quality and privacy concerns; S028 provides a governance representation rather than proof that sharing improves all networks.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The interfacial data and decisions relying on its meaning.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Data subjects, sending/receiving organisations and analysts.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Validate recorded events against how each organisation actually performs and records work.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** A semantic/interface agreement with examples; a comprehensive ontology is optional.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Interpretation errors, coordination failures and disclosure burden.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Reconcile the meanings of cases, events, messages and completion across interfaces while limiting disclosure to a justified purpose. Technical schema compatibility is not enough when organisations interpret the same field differently.; causal limit: S035 highlights event-data quality and privacy concerns; S028 provides a governance representation rather than proof that sharing improves all networks.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** Data custodians control authorised disclosure; process parties jointly resolve business meaning within their commitments.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Provide clarification or manual resolution for ambiguous messages.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Message receipt is distinct from agreement with its meaning or fulfilment of its obligation.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** Schema/meaning change, joining error, disclosure concern or incompatible completion claims.

Reconsider on: Schema/meaning change, joining error, disclosure concern or incompatible completion claims.; anti ceremony boundary: Presence of an artefact, role, meeting, score or tool does not establish the observable postcondition; removing it must not destroy a real protective or accountability function.

### EBPM-050 — Environmental and societal outcome constraints

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-050`. Disposition: `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/49`.

**Mature form.** Include material environmental and societal consequences in process purpose, boundaries and redesign comparisons. Identify who or what bears these effects and distinguish measured outcomes from reporting capability, commitments or maturity ratings.

**Controlled failure.** Greenwashing; burden shifting; reporting readiness mistaken for reduced impact; lifecycle omissions.

**Trigger.** A process has material resource, environmental or social consequences or a sponsor claims sustainable improvement.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** A focused material-impact assessment may suffice; do not add an ESG dashboard unrelated to a consequential decision.

**Prerequisites.** A defensible impact boundary, relevant evidence and authority to adjudicate conflicts with cost or speed.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A process has material resource, environmental or social consequences or a sponsor claims sustainable improvement.; consumer and authority: Sponsors and owners decide within legitimate constraints; affected parties and domain experts contribute consequences that internal metrics may omit.

**Authority boundary.** Sponsors and owners decide within legitimate constraints; affected parties and domain experts contribute consequences that internal metrics may omit.

**Required communication.** Explain impact assumptions, displaced burdens and conflicts rather than announcing all objectives simultaneously improved.

**Observable postcondition.** A sustainability claim states what changed, for whom, over which boundary and with what evidence limits.

**Criticism.** S031 is survey-based; S040 is a national cross-sectional association with ESG readiness; S032 is a doctoral research agenda. None establishes universal causal sustainability gains.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The process and materially affected environmental/social consequences beyond local activity counts.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Workers, recipients, communities, partners and environmental interests represented by appropriate expertise.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Inspect actual consumption and consequences, not only sustainability procedures or self-reports.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** An impact-boundary and alternatives account, proportionate to materiality.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Material environmental/social outcomes separately from reporting capability.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Include material environmental and societal consequences in process purpose, boundaries and redesign comparisons. Identify who or what bears these effects and distinguish measured outcomes from reporting capability, commitments or maturity ratings.; causal limit: S031 is survey-based; S040 is a national cross-sectional association with ESG readiness; S032 is a doctoral research agenda. None establishes universal causal sustainability gains.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** Sponsors and owners decide within legitimate constraints; affected parties and domain experts contribute consequences that internal metrics may omit.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Human exceptions may be needed where standard optimisation harms a vulnerable group.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Automation can move costs into infrastructure, data production or external labour rather than eliminate them.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** New impact evidence, displaced burden, changed objectives or unsupported sustainability claims.

### EBPM-051 — Process intelligence joined to accountable intervention

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-051`. Disposition: `ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/50`.

**Mature form.** Use analytical outputs as evidence for accountable inquiry and intervention, preserving data, causal and organisational limits. Distinguish descriptive pattern, predictive performance, feasible intervention and achieved outcome.

**Controlled failure.** Prediction sold as causation; repeated benchmark reuse mistaken for replication; accurate models with no feasible response.

**Trigger.** Analytics is proposed to diagnose or recommend process changes.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** A simple case review or direct measure may answer the decision without mining, prediction or simulation.

**Prerequisites.** Relevant data, reliable interpretation, an actionable mechanism and an authorised consumer.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. Analytics is proposed to diagnose or recommend process changes.; consumer and authority: Analysts and tools generate evidence or recommendations; process actors retain responsibility for actions and consequences.

**Authority boundary.** Analysts and tools generate evidence or recommendations; process actors retain responsibility for actions and consequences.

**Required communication.** Communicate uncertainty, omitted work, comparison conditions and the proposed decision use.

**Observable postcondition.** An analytical result is accepted only for a stated evidential role and its action/outcome claims are separately evaluated.

**Criticism.** S019’s causal-dependency account is bounded by its model and data assumptions; S036 explicitly spans levels beyond technical accuracy.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The observed process and the intervention boundary, which need not coincide.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Analysts, owners, performers, recipients and people absent from the data.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Trace data generation and missing work; observed traces are not exhaustive process reality.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** Representation chosen for the question: descriptive, predictive, causal or simulation.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Model performance and actual process outcomes reported separately.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Expert judgement may identify exceptions or unobserved mechanisms.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Recommendation generation does not authorise execution or prove external benefit.

### EBPM-052 — Independently checked AI-assisted modelling

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-052`. Disposition: `ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/51`.

**Mature form.** Use generated process artefacts as drafts that require independent checking of syntax, domain meaning, omitted work and purpose. Separate benchmark task success from truthful reconstruction of an organisation and from authority to execute the generated design.

**Controlled failure.** Hallucinated steps or authority, plausible omissions, benchmark leakage, automation bias and confidential-data exposure.

**Trigger.** A generative model is used to elicit, draft, explain or revise a process representation.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** Human modelling, a short narrative or a decision table may be cheaper and more reliable for small or sensitive tasks.

**Prerequisites.** Adequate source information, qualified review, protected data handling and an explicit intended use.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A generative model is used to elicit, draft, explain or revise a process representation.; consumer and authority: The model has no independent business authority; authorised people remain responsible for accepting representations and interventions.

**Authority boundary.** The model has no independent business authority; authorised people remain responsible for accepting representations and interventions.

**Required communication.** Reviewers receive the input basis, generated claims and unresolved ambiguities, not just a polished diagram.

**Observable postcondition.** Accepted content is checked against the task’s evidence and intended semantics; uncertainty and unsupported additions remain identifiable.

**Criticism.** S033 evaluates modelling tasks in a bounded benchmark; S034 is a manifesto. Neither demonstrates safe autonomous organisational redesign.

### EBPM-068 — Coupling warranted distinctions to authorised effects

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-068`. Disposition: `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/67`.

**Mature form.** Keep three linked but non-equivalent claims explicit: evidence supports a distinction about work; an identified actor is authorised and able to intervene; observed consequences support an outcome judgement. Require the missing link to be established before promoting one claim into another.

**Controlled failure.** Analysis without agency; authority without warrant; action without benefit; excessive documentation of trivial links.

**Trigger.** A model, analytic result or recommendation is proposed as sufficient justification for operational change or success.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** A small team can establish the links through a short case discussion, an existing decision-maker and direct outcome confirmation.

**Prerequisites.** Purpose-fit evidence, effective authority, feasible intervention and proportionate outcome observation.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A model, analytic result or recommendation is proposed as sufficient justification for operational change or success.; consumer and authority: Evidence producers do not acquire execution powers by analysis; authorised actors do not acquire factual correctness by holding office.

**Authority boundary.** Evidence producers do not acquire execution powers by analysis; authorised actors do not acquire factual correctness by holding office.

**Required communication.** Transfer the evidence, uncertainty and proposed action to the responsible actor, then return consequence evidence to the original judgement.

**Observable postcondition.** A claim of improvement can be traced through warranted observation, an actual authorised intervention and its observed result, with missing links explicit.

**Criticism.** The component distinctions have source support, but this exact composed condition has not been independently validated as a management programme.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The process plus the evidence-to-action-to-consequence relations used to manage it.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Analysts, performers, authorised decision-makers, recipients and external accountability consumers.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Evidence of a model, a decision and an outcome are separately observed; none substitutes for the others.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** A relational account with optional compact records, not a forced sequential workflow.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** The original process outcome and whether each inferential/operative link is actually established.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Keep three linked but non-equivalent claims explicit: evidence supports a distinction about work; an identified actor is authorised and able to intervene; observed consequences support an outcome judgement. Require the missing link to be established before promoting one claim into another.; causal limit: The component distinctions have source support, but this exact composed condition has not been independently validated as a management programme.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** Evidence producers do not acquire execution powers by analysis; authorised actors do not acquire factual correctness by holding office.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** A human may legitimately stop, override or request more evidence within an explicit authority boundary.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Generated or executable models neither grant authority nor establish external success.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** A broken evidence/action/consequence link, contradiction or changed authority.

**Evidence a later target must supply.** Evidence of a model, a decision and an outcome are separately observed; none substitutes for the others. Measure: The original process outcome and whether each inferential/operative link is actually established.

### EBPM-069 — Boundary-sensitive improvement acceptance

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-069`. Disposition: `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/68`.

**Mature form.** Accept an improvement claim only over its declared consequence boundary and protected outcomes. A local gain remains qualified when material receiving-side burdens are unknown; do not aggregate away a harmed group or silently change the boundary to rescue the claim.

**Controlled failure.** Infinite scope expansion, strategic nondisclosure, metric aggregation masking harm and excessive evaluation cost.

**Trigger.** A redesign appears beneficial on one unit’s measures or changes who bears work and risk.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** For a simple isolated process, recipient confirmation and a small burden check may suffice.

**Prerequisites.** Explicit affected-party scope, comparable outcomes and a legitimate adjudicator of unavoidable trade-offs.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A redesign appears beneficial on one unit’s measures or changes who bears work and risk.; consumer and authority: The party claiming benefit cannot unilaterally accept another party’s material burden as harmless.

**Authority boundary.** The party claiming benefit cannot unilaterally accept another party’s material burden as harmless.

**Required communication.** Exchange local gains and displaced costs with affected actors; preserve unresolved disagreements.

**Observable postcondition.** The benefit claim is accepted, rejected or bounded to what the evidence supports, including unknown external effects.

**Criticism.** This is an analytical acceptance condition, not evidence that all costs can be measured or all values reconciled.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The intervention and materially affected consequences, with explicit exclusions.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Beneficiaries, workers, partners and parties bearing displaced effort, risk or environmental effects.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Receiving-side and off-system evidence can challenge local records.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** A consequence-boundary and trade-off account rather than one scalar score.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Outcome and burden by affected party, with uncertainty retained.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Accept an improvement claim only over its declared consequence boundary and protected outcomes. A local gain remains qualified when material receiving-side burdens are unknown; do not aggregate away a harmed group or silently change the boundary to rescue the claim.; causal limit: This is an analytical acceptance condition, not evidence that all costs can be measured or all values reconciled.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** The party claiming benefit cannot unilaterally accept another party’s material burden as harmless.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** A justified exception may preserve a protected outcome despite lowering throughput.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Automated throughput gains cannot conceal scaled harm or external repair work.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** Displaced burden, scope change, new affected party or contradictory outcome evidence.

**Evidence a later target must supply.** Receiving-side and off-system evidence can challenge local records. Measure: Outcome and burden by affected party, with uncertainty retained.

**Anti-ceremony and retirement boundary.** protected function: The benefit claim is accepted, rejected or bounded to what the evidence supports, including unknown external effects.; consumer: The party claiming benefit cannot unilaterally accept another party’s material burden as harmless.; minimal adequate alternative: For a simple isolated process, recipient confirmation and a small burden check may suffice.; fuller form trigger: A redesign appears beneficial on one unit’s measures or changes who bears work and risk.; prerequisites: Explicit affected-party scope, comparable outcomes and a legitimate adjudicator of unavoidable trade-offs.; simplification risk: Infinite scope expansion, strategic nondisclosure, metric aggregation masking harm and excessive evaluation cost.; omission or retirement condition: Omit when the stated trigger is absent, an existing/cheaper mechanism supplies the required function, or its risk/consumer/dependency has disappeared. Reconsider on: Displaced burden, scope change, new affected party or contradictory outcome evidence.; anti ceremony boundary: Presence of an artefact, role, meeting, score or tool does not establish the observable postcondition; removing it must not destroy a real protective or accountability function.

**Conflicts and coupling.** Tensions: T01, T03 Relations: R039, R045, R049. Inspect their guards and incompatible assumptions in the composition model; no unlisted conflict is asserted absent merely because it was not registered.

### EBPM-070 — Minimal sufficient portfolio of BPM machinery

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-070`. Disposition: `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/69`.

**Mature form.** Select the smallest set of BPM mechanisms that jointly covers the actual purpose, evidence, authority and feedback needs. Evaluate shared functions and dependencies before adding artefacts, roles or tools; omit or retire machinery whose function is already adequately supplied or no longer needed.

**Controlled failure.** False economy removes needed evidence; simplicity becomes dogma; overlapping controls are removed without understanding defence or accountability functions.

**Trigger.** A BPM method, model, role, assessment or platform is proposed, expanded or retained.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** Use existing coordination and a narrative/case review when adequate; no new BPM-branded artefact is required.

**Prerequisites.** Knowledge of the protected function, consumer, existing coverage and cost of omission.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A BPM method, model, role, assessment or platform is proposed, expanded or retained.; consumer and authority: The accountable consumer and affected actors judge adequacy; method specialists do not mandate tools merely because they are standard practice.

**Authority boundary.** The accountable consumer and affected actors judge adequacy; method specialists do not mandate tools merely because they are standard practice.

**Required communication.** Explain which failure each selected mechanism controls and which existing mechanism can substitute for it.

**Observable postcondition.** The chosen configuration is coherent and justified, with omitted functions or unresolved dependencies explicit.

**Criticism.** S041 shows apparently cumbersome documentation may serve a contractual consumer; S010 shows recommended form alone does not establish quality. Minimality is conditional, not a universal size target.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The management machinery used for the selected process, not the whole organisation by default.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Method consumers, performers, decision-makers and those paying coordination/documentation costs.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Evaluate exercised functions rather than artefact presence.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** The lightest adequate representation for its users and decisions.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Protected outcomes and total coordination/evidence cost.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Select the smallest set of BPM mechanisms that jointly covers the actual purpose, evidence, authority and feedback needs. Evaluate shared functions and dependencies before adding artefacts, roles or tools; omit or retire machinery whose function is already adequately supplied or no longer needed.; causal limit: S041 shows apparently cumbersome documentation may serve a contractual consumer; S010 shows recommended form alone does not establish quality. Minimality is conditional, not a universal size target.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** The accountable consumer and affected actors judge adequacy; method specialists do not mandate tools merely because they are standard practice.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Simple arrangements must still support necessary discretion and escalation.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** No tool category is mandatory; additional automation requires its own trigger.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** Lost consumer, changed risk, duplicate coverage, excessive burden or inadequate observed performance.

**Evidence a later target must supply.** Evaluate exercised functions rather than artefact presence. Measure: Protected outcomes and total coordination/evidence cost.

### EBPM-071 — Coupled revision of representations and work

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-071`. Disposition: `RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/70`.

**Mature form.** Allow feedback to change a representation, the real work, the measure or the intervention hypothesis, but distinguish these acts. Review their consistency and continuity: updating a diagram does not change work, and changed work may require a revised account without implying improvement.

**Controlled failure.** Model laundering of bad practice; uncommunicated process change; evaluation criteria rewritten to declare success; loss of continuity.

**Trigger.** Consequences or exceptions challenge the current process account or a revision is proposed.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** A local correction to a note may suffice when work and authority are unchanged; avoid reopening the entire portfolio for a representational typo.

**Prerequisites.** Identified mismatch, actors authorised for the relevant kind of change and evidence of affected dependencies.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. Consequences or exceptions challenge the current process account or a revision is proposed.; consumer and authority: Modellers may revise descriptions within their remit; changing operational commitments or evaluation criteria requires the responsible authority.

**Authority boundary.** Modellers may revise descriptions within their remit; changing operational commitments or evaluation criteria requires the responsible authority.

**Required communication.** Tell performers and consumers what changed in work, representation, objectives or evidence and what remains unchanged.

**Observable postcondition.** The revision’s type and operational consequences are explicit, with critical outstanding obligations preserved.

**Criticism.** This synthesis is not a claim that BPM methods are automatically self-authorising or that feedback converges to a globally optimal process.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The process, its representations, measures and current change commitments.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Performers, modellers, owners, evaluators, recipients and partners affected by revision.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Maintain the distinction between altered accounts and altered practice.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** Version and purpose relationships sufficient for affected consumers, not mandatory elaborate configuration machinery.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Actual outcome, continuity and whether the revised account remains warranted.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Allow feedback to change a representation, the real work, the measure or the intervention hypothesis, but distinguish these acts. Review their consistency and continuity: updating a diagram does not change work, and changed work may require a revised account without implying improvement.; causal limit: This synthesis is not a claim that BPM methods are automatically self-authorising or that feedback converges to a globally optimal process.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** Modellers may revise descriptions within their remit; changing operational commitments or evaluation criteria requires the responsible authority.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Recurring exceptions can trigger redesign, but each departure is not automatically a new standard.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** A model update must not silently become permission for automated external action.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** Credible mismatch, recurring exception, changed objective or failed outcome hypothesis.

**Evidence a later target must supply.** Maintain the distinction between altered accounts and altered practice. Measure: Actual outcome, continuity and whether the revised account remains warranted.

Reconsider on: Credible mismatch, recurring exception, changed objective or failed outcome hypothesis.; anti ceremony boundary: Presence of an artefact, role, meeting, score or tool does not establish the observable postcondition; removing it must not destroy a real protective or accountability function.

### EBPM-072 — Governed low-code participation and delivery

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-072`. Disposition: `DOMAIN_SPECIFIC`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/71`.

**Mature form.** Match low-code users’ skills, support, permitted scope and release authority to the consequences of the applications they build. Distinguish a simpler modelling interface from validated competence to change shared processes or sensitive data use.

**Controlled failure.** Shadow applications, hidden dependencies, unsupported maintenance, overgeneralising developer usability to business-user competence.

**Trigger.** Non-specialists or decentralised teams build applications that affect process operation.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** Use a supported template or existing application; professional development may dominate where risk or integration complexity exceeds local capability.

**Prerequisites.** Task-fit language, training/support, bounded data/system access and accountable review of consequential delivery.

**Required communication.** Developers, users, support/IT and process owners communicate requirements, limits, dependencies and operational problems.

**Observable postcondition.** A delivered application has demonstrated task fit and an identified support/authority arrangement for its real consequences.

### EBPM-073 — Consistent declarative constraints and history-compatible change

Global key: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT::EBPM-073`. Disposition: `DOMAIN_SPECIFIC`. Examination: `EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT`. Full record: `EVOLVED_BUSINESS_PROCESS_MANAGEMENT_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json#/properties/72`.

**Mature form.** Where the chosen formalism supports it, check that the combined constraints admit appropriate executions and that a changed instance specification remains compatible with its recorded history. Separate formal satisfiability and dead-activity checks from the truth, completeness and legitimacy of the encoded constraints.

**Controlled failure.** Omitted obligations; wrong event mapping; satisfiable but harmful behaviour; state-space/constraint complexity; missing actual history.

**Trigger.** A declarative process has interacting constraints or is changed while cases are in progress.

**Non-trigger and cheap path.** For a few transparent constraints, competent manual checking may suffice; procedural or case-based alternatives may be easier for the actual users.

**Prerequisites.** A defined event alphabet and trace semantics, correct encoding, adequate history and a tractable analysis method.

**Consumer and decision.** decision: Whether and how to apply the mechanism under its trigger, or reject its universalised use. A declarative process has interacting constraints or is changed while cases are in progress.; consumer and authority: Analysts check encoded behaviour; accountable domain actors decide which constraints should apply and whether a departure is authorised.

**Authority boundary.** Analysts check encoded behaviour; accountable domain actors decide which constraints should apply and whether a departure is authorised.

**Required communication.** Explain conflicting constraints, blocked activities and history incompatibilities in terms domain actors can adjudicate.

**Observable postcondition.** Within the checked formal model, the stated consistency/history-compatibility result holds; no claim about unencoded harm or real-world correctness follows.

**Criticism.** S042 supplies formal reasoning and prototype evidence, not general deployment validation. S014 highlights the challenge of evolving knowledge-intensive contexts.

#### Domain profile

**PROCESS_PURPOSE_AND_BOUNDARY.** The encoded case traces and constraint set, not all organisational behaviour.

**BENEFICIARIES_AND_AFFECTED_ACTORS.** Constraint authors, case workers, recipients and parties whose obligations may be omitted.

**WORK_AS_DESCRIBED_VERSUS_WORK_AS_PERFORMED.** Recorded history must correspond sufficiently to actual events for a history check to have operational significance.

**MODEL_PURPOSE_AND_ABSTRACTION.** Formal declarative constraints and trace/automata semantics; explanations may use simpler domain examples.

**PERFORMANCE_OR_OUTCOME_MEASURE.** Formal consistency, possible/dead activities and compatible histories; operational outcomes require separate evidence.

**REDESIGN_HYPOTHESIS_AND_CAUSAL_LIMIT.** hypothesis: Where the chosen formalism supports it, check that the combined constraints admit appropriate executions and that a changed instance specification remains compatible with its recorded history. Separate formal satisfiability and dead-activity checks from the truth, completeness and legitimacy of the encoded constraints.; causal limit: S042 supplies formal reasoning and prototype evidence, not general deployment validation. S014 highlights the challenge of evolving knowledge-intensive contexts.

**PROCESS_OWNER_AND_EFFECTIVE_AUTHORITY.** Analysts check encoded behaviour; accountable domain actors decide which constraints should apply and whether a departure is authorised.

**HUMAN_DISCRETION_AND_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.** Optional versus mandatory constraints and authorised deviation must not be conflated with arbitrary discretion.

**AUTOMATION_AND_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_BOUNDARY.** Runtime flexibility is bounded by encoded semantics and execution implementation, not a licence for external action.

**FEEDBACK_AND_REVISION_TRIGGER.** Added/changed constraint, conflicting obligation, instance migration or unreliable history.

## Non-retained and unresolved candidates: no unconditional obligations

### EBPM-053 — Autonomous agentic process self-revision

**Disposition.** UNRESOLVED

**Reason.** The question was researched and a contemporary proposal inspected, but the evidence does not settle the asserted operational capability. The proposed extension remains unresolved rather than being promoted from agenda to established capability.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** Candidate proposition: agents could revise and enact organisational processes under goals and constraints. The inspected 2026 manifesto formulates research requirements; this study does not establish that autonomous cross-context self-revision is safe, effective or adequately governed.

**Evidence limit.** S034 is explicitly a research manifesto with open challenges; S030 supplies conceptual responsibility concerns, not a completed control solution.

**Relevant later question.** What evidence establishes safe authority-constrained revision and reliable outcome evaluation under changing objectives?

**Sources.** S030-L1, S030-L2, S034-L1, S034-L2

### EBPM-054 — Universal prescriptive standardisation

**Disposition.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Reason.** The unconditional formulation fails under source-supported or explicit analytical counterconditions; no blanket obligation is exported. The blanket proposition is rejected, narrowed or separated from its useful underlying function; the qualified mechanism remains in EBPM-036.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** No unconditional operating mechanism is retained. Knowledge-intensive and exceptional cases can require bounded judgement; a fixed route is not universally appropriate.

**Evidence limit.** Analytical objection grounded in the cited sources, not a claim that the entire tradition endorses this universalisation. S026 criticises bureaucratic formalism; S030 raises fairness and accountability. Neither proves every workaround is legitimate.

**Relevant later question.** Which case classes genuinely benefit from a mandatory sequence?

**Sources.** S012-L1, S012-L2, S014-L1, S014-L2, S026-L1, S030-L1, S030-L2, S042-L1, S042-L2

### EBPM-055 — Radical re-engineering as default

**Disposition.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Reason.** The unconditional formulation fails under source-supported or explicit analytical counterconditions; no blanket obligation is exported. The blanket proposition is rejected, narrowed or separated from its useful underlying function; the qualified mechanism remains in EBPM-030.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** No unconditional operating mechanism is retained. Disruption and transition costs can dominate, while incremental repair or no change may address the actual problem.

**Evidence limit.** Analytical objection grounded in the cited sources, not a claim that the entire tradition endorses this universalisation. S038 questions BPR novelty and unrealistic organisational claims; S027 records implementation losses rather than assuming deployment.

### EBPM-056 — Documentation or maturity score as success

**Disposition.** CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Reason.** The named artefact or observance can support a function, but its presence does not establish that function or its outcome. The blanket proposition is rejected, narrowed or separated from its useful underlying function; the qualified mechanism remains in EBPM-034.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** No unconditional operating mechanism is retained. Artefacts and self-assessed stages do not establish exercised capability or received outcomes.

S022 and S023 identify weak validation and prescriptive limits. S040 adds positive cross-sectional ESG-readiness associations but does not establish causal or environmental benefit.

**Disposition.** NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Reason.** This sufficiency/equivalence claim does not hold generally; the distinct useful mechanism is preserved in its linked qualified record. The blanket proposition is rejected, narrowed or separated from its useful underlying function; the qualified mechanism remains in EBPM-014.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** No unconditional operating mechanism is retained. Syntax, execution semantics, domain accuracy, authority and actual enactment are distinct.

### EBPM-058 — Automation as a sufficient improvement

**Disposition.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

**Reason.** The unconditional formulation fails under source-supported or explicit analytical counterconditions; no blanket obligation is exported. The blanket proposition is rejected, narrowed or separated from its useful underlying function; the qualified mechanism remains in EBPM-041.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** No unconditional operating mechanism is retained. Automation can amplify a flawed decision, shift manual repair or fail to be adopted.

**Evidence limit.** Analytical objection grounded in the cited sources, not a claim that the entire tradition endorses this universalisation. S029 is an agenda/editorial rather than a comparative effectiveness study; S027 shows implementation and outcomes cannot be presumed; S045 is exploratory platform analysis.

### EBPM-059 — Maximum utilisation as a universal goal

**Disposition.** NO_GENERAL_PROPERTY

**Reason.** This sufficiency/equivalence claim does not hold generally; the distinct useful mechanism is preserved in its linked qualified record. The blanket proposition is rejected, narrowed or separated from its useful underlying function; the qualified mechanism remains in EBPM-019.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** No unconditional operating mechanism is retained. Variability, bottlenecks and queues make utilisation alone an inadequate end-to-end objective; the relevant model assumptions must be checked.

**Disposition.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

### EBPM-063 — Universal fifty-element decomposition threshold

**Disposition.** SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM

**Reason.** The universal threshold formulation is replaced by purpose-, user- and semantics-sensitive complexity management. The blanket proposition is rejected, narrowed or separated from its useful underlying function; the qualified mechanism remains in EBPM-012.

**Narrower permissible interpretation.** No unconditional operating mechanism is retained. The 7PMG recommendation is not a universal discontinuity in comprehension or correctness; user, task, semantics and decomposition costs matter.

**Evidence limit.** Analytical objection grounded in the cited sources, not a claim that the entire tradition endorses this universalisation. S010’s small guideline experiment did not establish the tested benefit; S044’s positive notation redesign has one-platform/cohort limits. Simpler-looking is not universally better.

**Disposition.** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED

## Whole-system questions

Can the audit trace a consequential observation to a legitimate and capable decision-maker, an actual intervention or justified non-action, and evidence of received consequences? Which of those links is already supplied, inapplicable or uncertain? Does the selected process boundary conceal material work or burdens? Can affected participants challenge the observation and success criteria? Can the apparatus itself be simplified without losing its protective function? These are questions derived from EBPM-068–EBPM-071, not findings of a deficiency.

A later assessment must distinguish a representational amendment, an operational change, a change of objectives and a change of governance. It must not treat one as authorising the others. No particular repository, product or organisation has been examined here.

`SIBLING_CORPORA_NOT_CONSULTED`
